Since neither profile activates, ${toolsjar} remains undefined, causing Maven to fail\n   when trying to resolve the system-scoped dependency.\n\n6. The parent POM (auto-parent version 3) also has similar profiles but these don't help\n   since the dependency is declared in compile-testing's POM.\n\nFIX STRATEGY:\n-------------\nThe issue is in the parent POMs that need a profile for Java 9+. Since we cannot modify\nthe compile-testing-0.7 dependency (it's an external artifact), we need to add a profile\nto the auto-parent POM that:\n\n1.
